# this file, and libultra's own objects through libultra_map.json, whose names (the library's) the names here use
# for libultra and tools/rename.py leaves alone. Any other name can be renamed: tools/rename.py.

# name, image (game or boot), address, kind (func or data), size in bytes (- when unknown), and for a second name
# of a symbol, the name it shares the address with: a function that sees the symbol with another type or
# prototype than the shared declaration uses it (and the boot loader's copies of game functions use the game's
# names for the boot loader's symbols).
